{"forms":[{"form":"moon letters","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"antonyms":[{"word":"sun letter"}],"glosses":["One of fourteen Arabic letters that do not affect the pronunciation of the \"L\" of a preceding definite article (ال- (al--)). The fourteen moon letters are: ء (ʔ), ب (b), ج (j), ح (ḥ), خ (ḵ), ع (ʕ), غ (ḡ), ف (f), ق (q), ك (k), م (m), ه (h), و (w), ي (y)"],"synonyms":[{"word":"lunar letter"}],"topics":["human-sciences","linguistics","sciences"]}],"upstream_line":563181,"upstream_offset":2561872534,"upstream_record_sha256":"5fef3954ed8611a2dbfcafa5942a84c40a401ce05440752b451be8c1b87ce41e","word":"moon letter"}
